Maybe use nonconvex but can be solved via "successive convex approximation" (SCA): At current a, approximate b_k(y) by tangent (convex? quadratic indefinite, tangent is affine). Minimize max of affine functions (convex) subject sum=1, y≥0. This yields a descent step. Solve LP:
minimize t
s.t. ∑ y=1, y≥0,
for all k: b_k + ∇b_k(a)·(y-a) ≤ t.
This is convex (linear constraints). It uses cone of active/all constraints. If t < M, we get new y. This is better than active-only and accounts for approximate all constraints. It is SCA for nonconvex. Let's explore! Since b_k are quadratics, first-order model. At current a, define linearized coefficients ℓ_k(y)=b_k(a)+2? wait gradient wrt y: ∑∇b_k(a)·y - const. We include all k or top. Solve LP:
minimize t
variables y_i≥0, t.
s.t. ∑ y_i=1
∑_i g_{k,i} y_i ≤ t + C_k, where C_k = b_k(a) - ∑_i g_{k,i} a_i? But ∇b_k(a)·a = 2 b_k? Let's check b_k = a^T T_k a, ∇=2T_k a. Dot with a =2b_k. So linearized: b_k(y) ≈ b_k(a) + 2(T_k a)·(y-a) = 2g? If g=∇b, constraint: b_k + g_k·(y-a) ≤ t => g_k·y ≤ t + (b_k - g_k·a) = t - b_k (since g·a=2b). So b_k - 2b_k = -b_k. Wait sign: b_k + g·(y-a) = b_k + g·y - g·a = b_k + g·y -2b_k = g·y - b_k. So constraint g_k·y ≤ t + b_k? Actually want ≤t: g·y - b_k ≤t => g·y ≤ t + b_k. Good.
This is LP with n vars plus t, constraints 2n-1. y nonnegative sum1. Objective min t. If optimal t < M, new y likely reduces linearized max. Then line search? Since LP already at sum1, can set y. This is convex approximation (first-order). It may be very effective. It uses all coefficients or top K; constraints 639, variables 321, sparse 204k. Each LP cheap. Iterate: a←y (or blend). def make_slices(n):
    L=2*n-1
    sl=[]
    for k in range(L):
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        # start=k-lo, stop=k-hi-1, step=-1
        sl.append((lo,hi,k-lo,k-hi-1))

Loop implementation:
```
def grad_q(a,w,n):
    q=np.zeros(n)
    # unroll? loop idx
    for idx in range(n):
        ai=a[idx]
        if ai != 0.0:
            q[:n-idx] += ai * w[idx:idx+n-idx]
    return q
```

Let's outline helper functions in final code:
- `get_data(n)`:
```
data = {}
def data_for(n):
   if n in data: return data[n]
   L=2*n-1
   idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)[:,None]
   js = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)[None,:]
   rows = (idx + js).ravel().astype(np.int32)
   cols = js.ravel().astype(np.int32)
   idx_flat = idx.ravel().astype(np.int32)
   ones_n = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
   dt = {'n':n,'L':L,'rows':rows,'cols':cols,'idx':idx_flat}
   data[n]=dt; return dt
```
`idx.ravel()` order C: idx as (n,1), broadcast? Need ensure `idx` 2D `np.arange(n)[:,None]`, `js=np.arange(n)[None,:]`. `idx_b = np.broadcast_to(idx, (n,n)).ravel()` gives idx per row? We want idx dimension first (a index) and js columns. If idx shape (n,1), js (1,n), `(idx+js)` gives matrix element [i,j]=i+j. `idx.ravel()` default C gives row-major: for i=0 all j, then i=1 all j. That's what we want. `idx_flat = np.broadcast_to(idx, (n,n)).ravel()`. `cols = np.broadcast_to(js,(n,n)).ravel()`. Good.
- `build_A_scaled(a, dt, scale=2.0, with_t=False)`:
```
L=dt['L']; n=dt['n']
A = np.zeros((L, n + (1 if with_t else 0)), dtype=np.float64)
A[dt['rows'], dt['cols']] = scale * a[dt['idx']]
if with_t: A[:,n] = -1.0
return A
```
But for SCA we need -1 column even where no assignment. Set before or after:
```
A = np.empty((L,n+1)); A[:,n]=-1.0; A[:, :n] fill? If use zeros, okay.
```
For parent unscaled without t, A L×n.
- `fast_score(a, n)`:
```
if n is None n=len(a)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-15: return inf
b=np.convolve(a,a)
return float(2*n*b.max()/(s*s))
```

Detailed SCA function:
```
def sca_run(a, stop, max_iters=10000, mask_top=None):
    dt=get_data(n)
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    local = fast_score(a); update_best_a(a, best)
    stag=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop-2: break
       b=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(b.max())
       s=a.sum() # should1
       # if M <= local? local=score=2nM/s^2. Use M.
       a_lp = np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a_lp = a_lp/a_lp.sum()
       A_ub=build_A_scaled(a_lp, dt,scale=2.0,with_t=True)
       # optional mask top
       if use_mask:
          mask = b >= np.percentile(b, 90) ?
          A_ub=A_ub[mask]; b_ub=b[mask]
       else: b_ub=b.copy()
       b_ub += 1e-12
       c = np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
       A_eq = np.concatenate((np.ones(n), [0.0]))
       bounds = [(0,1)]*n + [(0, max(1.0, 2.0*M))]? t upper maybe 1. Use 1.0.
       try: res=linprog(...)
       except: res=None
       if res is None or not res.success: 
           # try with top 100? maybe
       y=res.x[:n]
       # due tolerance, clean
       y=np.clip(y,0,1); ys=y.sum()
       if ys>0: y=y/ys
       if np.linalg.norm(y-a) < 1e-10 or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): stag++; continue
       # line search
       best_lam=None; best_v=fast_score(a)
       for lam in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.03):
           cand=(1-lam)*a+lam*y
           csum=cand.sum()
           if csum<=0: continue
           cand=cand/csum
           # if any cand neg? y,a nonneg, no
           v=fast_score(cand)
           if v < best_v*(1-1e-12): best_v=v; best_lam=lam
       if best_lam is not None:
           a=(1-best_lam)*a+best_lam*y; a/=a.sum()
           local=fast_score(a)
           update_best(a)
           stag=0
       else:
           stag+=1
           if stag%20==0:
               # random log perturbation
               z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z+=0.05*rand; ap=softmax; if score improve accept
           if stag>80: break
    return a
```

L-BFGS objective gradient with q loop. Let's implement `smooth_min(a, stop, betas=None, max_inner=200)`:
```
def smooth_min(a_in, stop, max_iter_total=...):
   z = np.log(np.maximum(a_in,1e-12)); z -= z.mean()
   # scale z to [-10,10]? if large, shrink: z = np.tanh( (z - mean)/5 )*8? This changes shape? Could preserve logits? Better clip.
   z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)
   dt=get_data(n)
   for phase, beta in enumerate(betas):
       stop_phase = min(stop-30, time+allowed)
       def fun(z):
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100, zeros
           zz = z - max(z) # for exp; but gradient? softmax same. However if we shift inside, gradient w.r.t original z same? Since a depends on z-max, derivative ∂a/∂z = a(δ - a). If we compute using shifted but same a, formula using a works. Yes.
           e=np.exp(zz); a=e/e.sum()
           b=np.convolve(a,a)
           m=b.max()
           u=beta*(b-m); # <=0
           e2=np.exp(u); s=e2.sum()
           val=2*n*(m + math.log(s)/beta)
           w=e2/s
           q=np.zeros(n)
           for ii in range(n):
               ai=a[ii]
               if ai:
                   q[:n-ii] += ai * w[ii:ii+n-ii]
           ga=4.0*n*q
           # subtract mean projection
           ga -= np.dot(a, ga)
           gz = a*ga
           # tiny regularization to z
           reg=1e-10
           val += reg*np.dot(z,z)
           gz += 2*reg*z
           return val, gz
       callback = lambda x: time.monotonic() >= stop_phase
       res=minimize(fun, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-80,80)]*n, options={'maxiter':100,'maxfun':1000,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':50}, callback=callback)
       z=res.x
       a_new=softmax(z); a_new=normalize; update_best(a_new); if fast_score(a_new) < best local? set a_in
   return a_in
```

def convolve_auto(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n < 512: return np.convolve(a,a)
   N=1
   while N < 2*n-1: N*=2
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N); return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]

def _exact_eval(arr):

       try: return float(ev([float(x) for x in arr]))
       except: pass
    # fallback local
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    n=len(a); s=a.sum()

   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s<1e-15: return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)

Let's write `build_sca_matrix(dt, a)`:
```
def build_sca_matrix(dt, a):
    L=dt['L']; n=dt['n']
    A=np.zeros((L,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:,n] = -1.0
    A[dt['rows'], dt['cols']] = 2.0 * a[dt['idx']]
    return A
```
For parent LP matrix:
```
def build_parent_matrix(dt,a):
    A=np.zeros((L,n),dtype=np.float64)
    A[dt['rows'],dt['cols']] = a[dt['idx']]
    return A
```
Maybe we can build SCA from parent matrix scaled and t. But okay.

For top mask selection:
```
def get_mask(b, kind, k):
   L=len(b)
   if kind is None or k is None or k>=L: return slice(None)
   if k <=0: return slice(0,0)
   idx=np.argpartition(b, L-k)[-k:]  # top k unsorted
   return idx
```
`argpartition` fast. For reproducibility, okay. If k>L, all. For top 50. Note constraints order not important.

L-BFGS polish after SCA. It needs optimized a. We can implement `lbfgs_polish(a_init, stop, max_phases=4)`. Use `fast_m` for local. Let's write carefully.
```
def lbfgs_polish(a_init, stop):
    if minimize is None: return a
    n=len(a_init); a=_normalize(a_init); local_m=fast_m(a); consider(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
    # betas
    b=np.convolve(a,a); m=b.max(); spread=max(float(np.percentile(b,60)-np.median(b)),1e-12)
    # Actually use m - np.percentile(b,50)
    spread=max(m - float(np.median(b)), 1e-12)
    base_betas=[5,20,80,300]
    betas=[]
    for f in base_betas:
        beta=min(100000.0, f/spread)
        if not betas or beta != betas[-1]: betas.append(beta)
    for beta in betas:
       if time>=stop-15: break
       # time allow per phase
       phase_stop=min(stop-10, time.monotonic()+60) # maybe
       def fun(z, beta=beta): ...
       def cb(x): return time.monotonic() >= phase_stop
       try:
          res=minimize(fun, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(-80,80)]*n, options={'maxiter':150,'maxfun':800,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':40}, callback=cb)
          if res.x is not None: z=res.x
       except: pass
       a_new=softmax(z); a_new=normalize; v=fast_m(a_new); if v<local_m*(1-1e-12): a=a_new; local_m=v; consider(a)
       z=log(a); ... maybe recenter
    consider(a); return a
```
In fun, regularization tiny: `reg=1e-12`. But L-BFGS with regularization gradient may hinder? Use 0? Could drift. Since bounds, no drift. Remove regularization.

Softmax:
```
def softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float); z=z-np.max(z); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); return e/s
```

Let's write `get_data`:
```
_DATA = {}
def _data(n):
    n=int(n)
    d=_DATA.get(n)
    if d is None:
       L=2*n-1
       idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[:,None]
       js = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None,:]
       rows = (idx + js).ravel().astype(np.int32)
       cols = js.ravel().astype(np.int32)
       idxf = idx.ravel().astype(np.int32)
       # order: C row-major gives idx for i rows. Good.
       d={'n':n,'L':L,'rows':rows,'cols':cols,'idx':idxf}
       _DATA[n]=d
    return d
```
Memory for n=512: idx 262k int64 temporary 2MB, rows etc int32. Good.

`build_sca_matrix` uses idx. But for `A[rows, cols] = 2.0 * a[idx]`, `idx` int32; `a[idx]` float. Good.

Potential duplicate assignment? No. But in C row-major, for i (idx) 0..n-1, j 0..n-1. For fixed idx, rows idx+j. For fixed k, rows can have multiple idx. No same (k,j) for different idx because idx=k-j unique. Good.

Now, `interp_len`:
```
def _interp_len(a,L):
    if len(a)==L: return a.copy()
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(a)); y=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,L)
    out=np.interp(y,x,a); out=np.clip(out,0,None)
    s=out.sum(); if s>1e-15: out/=s
    else: out=np.ones(L); out/=L
    return out
```

def consider(a):
   nonlocal best_score,best_arr
   arr=_clean(a)
   if arr.size==0: return
   fs=_fast_score(arr)
   if not math.isfinite(fs): return
   if fs + 1e-11 < best_score:

       if math.isfinite(es) and es < best_score:

       best_m=local_m; best_c=None

       for lam in sams:

def _build_sca(dt,a):
   n=dt['n']; L=dt['L']
   idx=dt['idx']
   if n>400 and sps is not None:
      vals = np.empty(len(idx)+L, dtype=np.float64)
      vals[:len(idx)] = 2.0*a[idx]
      vals[len(idx):] = -1.0
      rows = np.empty(len(idx)+L, dtype=np.int32)
      rows[:len(idx)] = dt['rows']; rows[len(idx):]=dt['rangeL']
      cols = np.empty(len(idx)+L, dtype=np.int32)
      cols[:len(idx)] = dt['cols']; cols[len(idx):]=dt['tcol']
      return sps.csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)), shape=(L,n+1))
   else:
      A=np.zeros((L,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
      A[:,n] = -1.0
      A[dt['rows'], dt['cols']] = 2.0*a[idx]
      return A
```

```
    def line_search(a, y, local_m):
        best_m=local_m; best_c=None
        sams=(1.0,0.85,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.03)
        for lam in sams:
           cand=(1-lam)*a + lam*y
           cs=cand.sum()
           if cs<=1e-15: continue
           cand=cand/cs
           m=_m(cand)
           if m < best_m*(1.0 - 1e-12):
               best_m=m; best_c=cand
        return best_c,best_m
```
Maybe if y all zero? skip. If y close a, m same.

`perturb`:
```
    def perturb(a, scale):
        n=a.size
        if n<=1: return a.copy()
        z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
        z=z-z.mean()
        z+=scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
        z-=z.mean()
        return _softmax(z)
```

SCA function:
```
    def sca_run(a_init, stop, max_iters=50000):
        a=_normalize(a_init.copy())
        if a.size==0: return a
        n=a.size
        if n ==1: return a
        if linprog is None: return a
        dt=_data(n)
        local_m=_m(a); consider(a)
        stag=0
        lam_cycle=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1,0.03)
        eq = np.concatenate((np.ones(n), np.array([0.0])))
        c = np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
        bounds_y = [(0.0,1.0)]*n + [(0.0,1.0)]
        max_mask_choice = min(??)
        for it in range(max_iters):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - max(2.0, 0.02*n): break
            b=np.convolve(a,a)
            L=b.size
            M=float(b.max())
            if not math.isfinite(M): break
            # choose mask
            if stag < 12: kmask=None
            elif stag < 24: kmask=min(200,L)
            elif stag < 40: kmask=min(100,L)
            elif stag < 70: kmask=min(50,L)
            else: kmask=min(25,L)
            a_for=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a_for_sum=a_for.sum(); a_for=a_for/a_for.sum()
            A_ub=_build_sca(dt,a_for)
            if kmask is not None and kmask < L:
                rowidx=np.argpartition(b, L-kmask)[-kmask:]
                # if kmask small, fixed shape? For sparse, rowidx indexing okay? CSR row index with array returns sparse matrix. Yes.
                A_ub=A_ub[rowidx]
                b_ub=b[rowidx] + 1e-9
            else:
                b_ub=b + 1e-9
            try:
                res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=eq, b_eq=1.0, bounds=bounds_y, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
            except Exception:
                res=None
            y=None
            if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False) and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
                y=np.asarray(res.x[:n],dtype=np.float64)
                y=np.clip(y,0,1.0)
                ys=y.sum()
                if ys>1e-15:
                    y=y/ys
                else: y=None
            if y is not None and float(np.sum((y-a)**2)) > 1e-16:
                cand, nm = line_search(a, y, local_m)
                if cand is not None and nm < local_m:
                    a=cand; local_m=nm; consider(a); stag=0
                    continue
            # no improvement
            stag += 1
            if stag % 12 == 0:
                improved=False
                for scale in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35):
                    ap=perturb(a,scale)
                    mp=_m(ap)
                    if mp < local_m*(1-1e-12):
                        a=ap; local_m=mp; consider(a); stag=0; improved=True; break
                if not improved and stag > 100:
                    break
            elif stag > 120:
                break
        consider(a)
        return a
```

Parent LP:
```
    def parent_lp_run(a_init, stop, max_iters=5000):
        a=_normalize(a_init.copy()); n=a.size
        if n==1 or linprog is None: return a
        dt=_data(n); local_m=_m(a); consider(a); stag=0
        c = -np.ones(n)
        margins=(1.0,0.995,0.99,0.98,0.95)
        for it in range(max_iters):
           if time.monotonic() >= stop - max(2,0.02*n): break
           b=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(b.max())
           if not finite: break
           a_for=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a_for/=sum
           A=_build_parent(dt,a_for)
           ubg=max(1.0, 2.0/max(float(a_for.max()),1e-12))
           bounds=[(0.0, ubg)]*n
           improved=False
           for mm in margins:
               if time.monotonic() >= stop - max(1,0.01*n): break
               b_ub=M*mm+1e-9
               try: res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
               except: res=None
               if res and success and res.x is not None:
                   g=np.clip(np.asarray(res.x[:n]),0,None)
                   gs=g.sum()
                   if gs>1e-15: g/=gs; cand,nm=line_search(a,g,local_m); if cand improve: accept; improved=True; break
           if improved: stag=0
           else:
               stag+=1; if stag%15==0 random perturb...
               if stag>80: break
        consider(a); return a
```
Note parent LP objective max sum, if margin=1 and feasible a, optimum may have G finite. `ubg` maybe 640. Good.

Random perturb in parent same.

L-BFGS:
```
    def lbfgs_polish(a_init, stop, max_phases=4):
        a=_normalize(a_init.copy()); n=a.size
        if n==1 or minimize is None: return a
        local_m=_m(a); consider(a)
        z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z=z-z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
        b=np.convolve(a,a); m=float(b.max())
        med=float(np.median(b))
        spread=max(m-med, 1e-12)
        beta_list=[]
        for f in (5,20,80,300):
            beta= min(150000.0, f/spread)
            if not beta_list or beta > beta_list[-1]*1.5:
                beta_list.append(beta)
        rem=stop-time.monotonic()
        per=max(10.0, rem/max(1,len(beta_list)))*0.9
        for beta in beta_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop-10: break
            phase_stop = min(stop-5, time.monotonic()+per)
            # objective uses n from a.size
            def fun(z, beta=beta):
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
                zz=z - z.max()
                e=np.exp(zz); s=e.sum()
                if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
                aa=e/s
                bb=np.convolve(aa,aa)
                mm=float(bb.max())
                u=beta*(bb-mm)
                np.clip(u, -745, 0, out=u)
                eu=np.exp(u); se=eu.sum()
                if se<=0: se=1
                lse=mm + math.log(se)/beta
                w=eu/se
                q=np.zeros(n)
                # loop
                for ii in range(n):
                    ai=aa[ii]
                    if ai:
                        q[:n-ii] += ai * w[ii:ii+n-ii]
                ga=2.0*q
                ga -= float(np.dot(aa,ga))
                gz=aa*ga
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)): gz=np.zeros(n)
                return lse, gz
            def cb(x): return time.monotonic() >= phase_stop
            try:
                res=minimize(fun,z,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=[(-80,80)]*n,options={'maxiter':120,'maxfun':600,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':40},callback=cb)
                if res is not None and res.x is not None: z=res.x
            except Exception: pass
            an=_softmax(z); an=_normalize(an)
            mn=_m(an)
            if mn < local_m*(1-1e-12):
                a=an; local_m=mn; consider(a)
            z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z=z-z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
        consider(a); return a
```
Potential in `fun`: `z.max()` if z contains nan; checked finite. If all z equal large, stable. `math.log(se)` safe. `u=beta*(bb-mm)` could overflow if beta* value >745? We set clip out, but if beta* negative < -745 error? `np.clip` can handle array but if product overflow to -inf? beta up to 150k, bb-mm up to maybe 1? product -150k no overflow; fine. Use `u = np.clip(beta * (bb - mm), -700.0, 0.0)`.

`ga -= dot` and no regularization. The gradient may be tiny if at min. Good.

In L-BFGS, `n` captured from a initial. If a changes length? no.

Random start phase:
```
    def random_starts(stop, iteration_cap=20):
       nonlocal? consider updates best
       base=_normalize(best_arr.copy()) if best_arr is not None else np.ones(...)
       n=base.size
       seqs=[base]
       # perturb
       for sc in (0.05,0.15,0.3): seqs.append(perturb(base,sc))
       # ramps
       x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/max(1,n-1)
       for cf in (-0.5,0.5,1.0):
           w=base*np.exp(cf*x); w=_normalize(w); seqs.append(w)
       # maybe reverse? base[::-1]
       seqs.append(base[::-1].copy())
       # unique? no
       for s in seqs:
          if time.monotonic() >= stop-5: break
          sm=math.sqrt
          t_allow=min(18.0, stop-time.monotonic()-5)
          if t_allow<5: continue
          v=_fast_score(s)
          if v > best_score*1.5: continue  # skip too bad
          w=sca_run(s, time.monotonic()+t_allow, max_iters=80)
          # maybe quick L-BFGS if time
       return
```
But `best_score*1.5`: if best_score inf? no. If base_score 1.51, 1.5* ~2.26, ramp maybe. If best_score near target, skip starts >2.26; okay.

Length search:
```
    def length_search(stop):
       base=best_arr if best_arr is not None else np.ones(320)
       base=np.asarray(base,dtype=float).ravel()
       base_n=base.size
       cands=[]
       for L in [base_n, base_n-8, base_n-4, base_n-2, base_n+2, base_n+4, base_n+8, 256, 320, 384, 448, 512]:
           if 32 <= L <= 512 and L not in cands: cands.append(L)
       cands.sort(key=lambda L: abs(L-base_n))
       for L in cands:
          if time.monotonic() >= stop-5: break
          t_allow = min(25.0, stop-time.monotonic()-5)
          if t_allow < 8: break
          init=_interp_len(base,L)
          w=sca_run(init, time.monotonic()+t_allow, max_iters=50)
          # If improved and time allow, lbfgs short?
          if time.monotonic() < stop-10:
              # maybe run small SCA again? Not.
          consider(w)
```

    now=time.monotonic()
    # Ensure best finite

    # Phase 1 main SCA until deadline-260 or now+650
    stop_main = min(deadline - 260.0, now + 650.0)
    if stop_main > now + 5:
        a_main = sca_run(a_main, stop_main, max_iters=50000)
    # Phase 2 parent LP
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_p = min(deadline - 180.0, now + 100.0)
    if stop_p > now + 5 and linprog is not None:
        a2=parent_lp_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_p, max_iters=5000)
    # Phase 3 SCA best
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_sca2 = min(deadline - 160.0, now + 70.0)
    if stop_sca2 > now + 5:
        a3=sca_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_sca2, max_iters=10000)
    # Phase 4 random starts
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_rand = min(deadline - 120.0, now + 80.0)
    if stop_rand > now + 10:
        random_starts(stop_rand)
    # Phase 5 length
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_len = min(deadline - 80.0, now + 80.0)
    if stop_len > now + 10:
        length_search(stop_len)
    # Phase 6 L-BFGS final
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_lbf = min(deadline - 15.0, now + 90.0)
    if stop_lbf > now + 10:
        a_l=lbfgs_polish(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_lbf, max_phases=4)
    # Phase 7 final SCA small
    now=time.monotonic()
    stop_final = deadline - 5.0
    if stop_final > now + 8:
        a_f=sca_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_final, max_iters=200)
    # Final variants: sparsify/sym
    if best_arr is not None:
        base=_normalize(best_arr.copy())
        for th in (1e-5,1e-4,1e-3):
            v=base.copy(); v[v<th]=0; if v.sum()>1e-15: consider(v)
        sym=_normalize((base+base[::-1])/2); consider(sym)
        rev=base[::-1].copy(); consider(rev)
    # Ensure best
    if best_arr is None:
       best_arr=np.ones(320); consider(best_arr)
    final=_clean(best_arr)
    if final.size==0 or not math.isfinite(_fast_score(final)): final=np.ones(320)
    s=final.sum(); if s<0.01: final=np.ones(320)
    final=np.clip(final,0,1000)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

```
def hypergeo(n, m=1.0):
   a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
   for k in range(1,n):
       c=0.0
       # c_k = sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}
       # We can compute O(n^2) by loop, n=320 fine.
       for i in range(1,k): c += a[i]*a[k-i]
       a[k]=max(0,(m-c)*0.5) # since a0=1, 2a0=2
   a/=a.sum()
```

Implement `_clean` with finite filter:
```
def _clean(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return arr
    arr=arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size==0: return arr
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    return arr
```

In `random_starts`, `v = _fast_score(s)`; if best_score inf? no. If v > best_score*1.5 and best_score finite. If best_score maybe 1.5, 1.5*1.5=2.25. Good. If start score inf, skip.

`random_starts` includes hypergeometric generation. Need define helper inside or global. Let's define global `_hypergeo(n)`.
```
def _hypergeo(n):
    a=np.zeros(n)
    if n<=0: return np.ones(1)
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
       if k>1:
          c=float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[1:k][::-1]))
       else: c=0.0
       val=0.5*(1.0-c)
       a[k]=val if val>0 else 0.0
    s=a.sum()
    if s>1e-15: a/=s
    else: a=np.ones(n); a/=n
    return a
```
For n=320, O(n^2) dot slices; okay. But `a[1:k]` length up to319, dot. Good. For k large, c can become >1? val zero. Could make subsequent c? If zeros, okay. Score maybe.

    ii=np.arange(n,1.0)
    w=np.exp(-alpha*np.log(ii)); # or ii**-alpha
```

In random_starts, avoid duplicate starts? Not necessary. But if many and time, could. Use a list and maybe `seqs.append` but no uniqueness.

Potential issue: `_fast_score` on `base[::-1]` same; skip if not improve. But SCA on reverse same basin? no need.

```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
    import scipy.sparse as sps
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    minimize = None
    sps = None

TARGET = 1.50274349
_DATA = {}


def _clean(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def _normalize(a):
    arr = _clean(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _m(a):
    a = _clean(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        return float(b.max()) / (s * s)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _fast_score(a):
    n = np.asarray(a).size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = _m(a)
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _exact_eval(a):
    arr = _clean(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if callable(ev):
        try:
            return float(ev([float(x) for x in arr]))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return _fast_score(arr)


def _interp_len(a, L):
    a = _clean(a)
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    if a.size == L:
        return _normalize(a)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    out = np.interp(y, x, a)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        out = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        out = out / s
    return out


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        e = np.ones_like(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
    return e / s


def _hypergeo(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        if k > 1:
            c = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[1:k][::-1]))
        else:
            c = 0.0
        val = 0.5 * (1.0 - c)
        a[k] = val if val > 0.0 else 0.0
    return _normalize(a)


def _data(n):
    n = int(n)
    d = _DATA.get(n)
    if d is not None:
        return d
    L = 2 * n - 1
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
    js = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
    rows = (idx + js).ravel().astype(np.int32)
    cols = js.ravel().astype(np.int32)
    idxf = idx.ravel().astype(np.int32)
    full_rows = np.concatenate((rows, np.arange(L, dtype=np.int32)))
    full_cols = np.concatenate((cols, np.full(L, n, dtype=np.int32)))
    d = {
        "n": n,
        "L": L,
        "rows": rows,
        "cols": cols,
        "idx": idxf,
        "full_rows": full_rows,
        "full_cols": full_cols,
        "m": int(rows.size),
    }
    _DATA[n] = d
    return d


def _build_sca(dt, a):
    n = dt["n"]
    L = dt["L"]
    idx = dt["idx"]
    if n > 400 and sps is not None:
        vals = np.empty(dt["m"] + L, dtype=np.float64)
        vals[:dt["m"]] = 2.0 * a[idx]
        vals[dt["m"]:] = -1.0
        return sps.csr_matrix(
            (vals, (dt["full_rows"], dt["full_cols"])),
            shape=(L, n + 1),
        )
    A = np.zeros((L, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:, n] = -1.0
    A[dt["rows"], dt["cols"]] = 2.0 * a[idx]
    return A


def _build_parent(dt, a):
    n = dt["n"]
    L = dt["L"]
    idx = dt["idx"]
    if n > 400 and sps is not None:
        return sps.csr_matrix(
            (a[idx], (dt["rows"], dt["cols"])),
            shape=(L, n),
        )
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A[dt["rows"], dt["cols"]] = a[idx]
    return A


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    reserve = 20.0 if budget > 60.0 else min(5.0, 0.2 * budget)
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        arr = _clean(a)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        fs = _fast_score(arr)
        if not math.isfinite(fs):
            return
        if fs + 1e-11 < best_score:
            es = _exact_eval(arr)
            if math.isfinite(es) and es < best_score:
                best_score = es
                best_arr = arr.copy()

    def line_search(a, y, local_m):
        best_m = local_m
        best_c = None
        for lam in (1.0, 0.85, 0.6, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1, 0.03):
            cand = (1.0 - lam) * a + lam * y
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            m = _m(cand)
            if m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                best_m = m
                best_c = cand
        return best_c, best_m

    def perturb(a, scale):
        n = a.size
        if n <= 1:
            return a.copy()
        z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
        z -= float(z.mean())
        z += scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
        z -= float(z.mean())
        return _softmax(z)

    def sca_run(a_init, stop, max_iters=50000):
        a = _normalize(a_init)
        n = a.size
        if n <= 1 or a.size == 0 or linprog is None:
            return a
        dt = _data(n)
        local_m = _m(a)
        consider(a)

        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[-1] = 1.0
        eq = np.concatenate((np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)))
        bounds_y = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(0.0, 1.0)]
        safe_margin = max(10.0, 0.05 * n)
        stag = 0

        for _ in range(max_iters):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - safe_margin:
                break
            b = np.convolve(a, a)
            L = b.size
            if L == 0:
                break
            M = float(b.max())
            if not math.isfinite(M):
                break

            if stag < 12:
                kmask = None
            elif stag < 24:
                kmask = min(200, L)
            elif stag < 40:
                kmask = min(100, L)
            elif stag < 70:
                kmask = min(50, L)
            else:
                kmask = min(25, L)

            a_for = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
            s_for = float(a_for.sum())
            if s_for <= 1e-15:
                a_for = np.ones_like(a) / a.size
            else:
                a_for = a_for / s_for

            A_ub = _build_sca(dt, a_for)
            if kmask is not None and kmask > 0 and kmask < L:
                rowidx = np.argpartition(b, L - kmask)[-kmask:]
                A_ub = A_ub[rowidx]
                b_ub = b[rowidx] + 1e-9
            else:
                b_ub = b + 1e-9

            res = None
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=eq,
                    b_eq=1.0,
                    bounds=bounds_y,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True},
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            y = None
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                y = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
                ys = float(y.sum())
                if ys > 1e-15:
                    y = y / ys
                else:
                    y = None

            if y is not None and float(np.sum((y - a) ** 2)) > 1e-16:
                cand, nm = line_search(a, y, local_m)
                if cand is not None and nm < local_m:
                    a = cand
                    local_m = nm
                    consider(a)
                    stag = 0
                    continue

            stag += 1
            if stag % 12 == 0:
                improved = False
                for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
                    ap = perturb(a, scale)
                    mp = _m(ap)
                    if mp < local_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                        a = ap
                        local_m = mp
                        consider(a)
                        stag = 0
                        improved = True
                        break
                if not improved and stag > 100:
                    break
            elif stag > 120:
                break

        consider(a)
        return a

    def parent_lp_run(a_init, stop, max_iters=5000):
        a = _normalize(a_init)
        n = a.size
        if n <= 1 or a.size == 0 or linprog is None:
            return a
        dt = _data(n)
        local_m = _m(a)
        consider(a)

        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        margins = (1.0, 0.995, 0.99, 0.98, 0.95)
        safe_margin = max(10.0, 0.05 * n)
        stag = 0

        for _ in range(max_iters):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - safe_margin:
                break
            b = np.convolve(a, a)
            M = float(b.max())
            if not math.isfinite(M):
                break

            a_for = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
            s_for = float(a_for.sum())
            if s_for <= 1e-15:
                a_for = np.ones_like(a) / a.size
            else:
                a_for = a_for / s_for

            A = _build_parent(dt, a_for)
            ubg = max(1.0, 2.0 / max(float(a_for.max()), 1e-12))
            bounds = [(0.0, ubg)] * n
            improved = False

            for mm in margins:
                if time.monotonic() >= stop - safe_margin:
                    break
                b_ub = M * mm + 1e-9
                res = None
                try:
                    res = linprog(
                        c,
                        A_ub=A,
                        b_ub=b_ub,
                        bounds=bounds,
                        method="highs",
                        options={"presolve": True},
                    )
                except Exception:
                    res = None

                if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
                    gs = float(g.sum())
                    if gs > 1e-15:
                        g = g / gs
                        cand, nm = line_search(a, g, local_m)
                        if cand is not None and nm < local_m:
                            a = cand
                            local_m = nm
                            consider(a)
                            improved = True
                            break

            if improved:
                stag = 0
            else:
                stag += 1
                if stag % 15 == 0:
                    improved = False
                    for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
                        ap = perturb(a, scale)
                        mp = _m(ap)
                        if mp < local_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                            a = ap
                            local_m = mp
                            consider(a)
                            stag = 0
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if not improved and stag > 80:
                        break
                elif stag > 100:
                    break

        consider(a)
        return a

    def lbfgs_polish(a_init, stop, max_phases=4):
        a = _normalize(a_init)
        n = a.size
        if n <= 1 or a.size == 0 or minimize is None:
            return a
        local_m = _m(a)
        consider(a)

        z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
        z -= float(z.mean())
        z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)

        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        m = float(b.max())
        med = float(np.median(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        spread = max(m - med, 1e-12)

        beta_list = []
        for f in (5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0):
            beta = min(150000.0, f / spread)
            if not beta_list or beta > beta_list[-1] * 1.5:
                beta_list.append(beta)
        beta_list = beta_list[:max_phases]

        rem = max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())
        per = max(10.0, rem / max(1, len(beta_list))) * 0.9

        for beta in beta_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 10.0:
                break
            phase_stop = min(stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + per)

            def fun(z, beta=beta):
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                zz = z - float(z.max())
                e = np.exp(zz)
                se = float(e.sum())
                if se <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(se):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                aa = e / se
                bb = np.convolve(aa, aa)
                mm = float(bb.max())
                if not math.isfinite(mm):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                u = np.clip(beta * (bb - mm), -700.0, 0.0)
                eu = np.exp(u)
                s2 = float(eu.sum())
                if s2 <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s2):
                    s2 = 1.0
                lse = mm + math.log(s2) / beta
                w = eu / s2

                q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                # q[j] = sum_i a[i] * w[i + j]
                for ii in range(n):
                    ai = aa[ii]
                    if ai != 0.0:
                        q += ai * w[ii:ii + n]

                ga = 2.0 * q
                ga -= float(np.dot(aa, ga))
                gz = aa * ga
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                    gz = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                return lse, gz

            def cb(x):
                return time.monotonic() >= phase_stop

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fun,
                    z,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=[(-80.0, 80.0)] * n,
                    options={"maxiter": 120, "maxfun": 600, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 40},
                    callback=cb,
                )
                if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    z = res.x
            except Exception:
                pass

            an = _softmax(z)
            an = _normalize(an)
            mn = _m(an)
            if mn < local_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                a = an
                local_m = mn
                consider(a)

            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
            z -= float(z.mean())
            z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)

        consider(a)
        return a

    def random_starts(stop):
        if best_arr is None:
            return
        base = _normalize(best_arr.copy())
        n = base.size
        if n <= 1:
            return
        seqs = [base]

        for sc in (0.05, 0.15, 0.30):
            seqs.append(perturb(base, sc))

        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
        for cf in (-0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
            w = base * np.exp(cf * x)
            seqs.append(_normalize(w))

        if n <= 512:
            seqs.append(_hypergeo(n))

        for alpha in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7):
            ii = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
            w = np.power(ii, -alpha)
            seqs.append(_normalize(w))

        for s in seqs:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 5.0:
                break
            t_allow = min(18.0, stop - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
            if t_allow < 5.0:
                continue
            v = _fast_score(s)
            if math.isfinite(best_score) and v > best_score * 1.5:
                continue
            w = sca_run(s, time.monotonic() + t_allow, max_iters=80)
            consider(w)

    def length_search(stop):
        if best_arr is None:
            return
        base = _clean(best_arr).copy()
        if base.size == 0:
            return
        base_n = base.size
        target_lengths = [
            base_n,
            base_n - 8,
            base_n - 4,
            base_n - 2,
            base_n + 2,
            base_n + 4,
            base_n + 8,
            256,
            320,
            384,
            448,
            512,
        ]
        cands = []
        for L in target_lengths:
            if 32 <= int(L) <= 512 and int(L) != base_n and int(L) not in cands:
                cands.append(int(L))
        if not cands:
            return
        cands.sort(key=lambda L: abs(L - base_n))

        for L in cands:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop - 5.0:
                break
            t_allow = min(25.0, stop - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
            if t_allow < 8.0:
                break
            init = _interp_len(base, L)
            w = sca_run(init, time.monotonic() + t_allow, max_iters=60)
            consider(w)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = _clean(h)
            if p.size > 0:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        consider(parent)

    if best_arr is None:
        p = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
        consider(p)

    if best_arr is None:
        p = _hypergeo(320)
        consider(p)

    if best_arr is None:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        p[0] = 1.0
        consider(p)

    if best_arr is None:
        return [1.0]

    a_main = _normalize(best_arr.copy())

    stop_main = min(deadline - 260.0, time.monotonic() + 650.0)
    if stop_main > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        a_main = sca_run(a_main, stop_main, max_iters=50000)

    stop_p = min(deadline - 180.0, time.monotonic() + 100.0)
    if stop_p > time.monotonic() + 5.0 and linprog is not None:
        a_main = parent_lp_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_p, max_iters=5000)

    stop_sca2 = min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
    if stop_sca2 > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        a_main = sca_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_sca2, max_iters=10000)

    stop_rand = min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
    if stop_rand > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        random_starts(stop_rand)

    stop_len = min(deadline - 80.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
    if stop_len > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        length_search(stop_len)

    stop_lbf = min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
    if stop_lbf > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        a_main = lbfgs_polish(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_lbf, max_phases=4)

    stop_final = deadline - 5.0
    if stop_final > time.monotonic() + 8.0:
        a_main = sca_run(_normalize(best_arr.copy()), stop_final, max_iters=20000)

    if best_arr is not None:
        base = _normalize(best_arr.copy())
        for th in (1e-5, 1e-4, 1e-3):
            v = base.copy()
            v[v < th] = 0.0
            if v.sum() > 1e-15:
                consider(v / v.sum())
        sym = _normalize((base + base[::-1]) * 0.5)
        consider(sym)
        consider(base[::-1].copy())
        consider(_hypergeo(base.size) if base.size <= 512 else base)

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
        consider(best_arr)

    final = _clean(best_arr)
    if final.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(_fast_score(final)):
        final = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
    if float(final.sum()) <= 0.01:
        final = np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    consider(final)
    if best_arr is not None and _clean(best_arr).size > 0:
        final = _clean(best_arr)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
